# topk_minmax: per-query RCA for run 37758359536

Read-only artifact and source analysis. Baseline Comet `f80042f78715e5a009352f1582f6c246a5f96131` / Apache Iceberg `af1da4c5bc86178c38c1c6db0578bdd9fb7021e3`; measured candidate Comet `92bba61b68ec44f305e0519f507cd02ae4546ee0` / fork Iceberg `949cc88acb6d5e5d47e4a4362075c8bc5afb8314`. Inspected current Comet `8cd4d9a98df4171c4554d0170b3c2618525657fe` and Iceberg `157cf6475ef46f272366d881580715e7edacf4c4`; neither checkout changed. No build, test, benchmark, or repository Python was executed. Only isolated standard-library JSON/text parsing was used; writes are report artifacts in this directory.

85 queries; 5,440 timed native records and 10,880 native warmup records. Every query has all four variants × eight JVM rounds × two timed repetitions and four validated warmups/round; full-result checksums match the plain Spark oracle exactly. Every captured SQL digest agrees across variants. Native coverage matches for 76/85 queries. Exact fixture results are evidence for these inputs, not a universal correctness proof.

Classification counts: pruning-win=42, expected-parity=19, fixed-overhead=0, regression=0, under-delivers=15, suspicious=0, coverage-diff=9. A primary regression classification takes precedence over under-delivery; CSV also carries an independent under_delivery flag. Expected-parity describes the pruning/control mechanism and can coexist with a large non-pruning engine improvement. Suspicious is reserved for unresolved equivalence/correctness concerns; a checked >10x speedup alone is insufficient.

## Evidence and interpretation

- Reused `/tmp/rp-bench-r3/matched-topk_minmax/`, downloaded artifacts named `matched-topk_minmax`; raw timings, checksums, diagnostic deltas and every SQL stage metric live in `results-topk_minmax.jsonl`, with `warmup-validation-topk_minmax.jsonl` for warmups. `manifests/topk_minmax-<variant>-<round>.json` enumerates SQL digests and repetition counts. `plans/topk_minmax/<variant>/<sql_sha256>.txt` contains SQL and the captured Spark physical tree. `topk_minmax-ledger.csv` preserves paths, all scalar SQL metric medians and JSON per-operator/per-scan distributions. `join-topk-evidence.json` preserves min/median/max timing/metric distributions and paired-round ratios.
- `data-topk_minmax.json` contains byte sizes and SHA-256 for data, delete and metadata files. `environment-topk_minmax.json`, `resolved/revisions.json`, and `/tmp/rp-bench-r3/resolved-revisions/revisions.json` record provenance/settings. `run-topk_minmax-<round>-<variant>.log.gz` contains Spark scheduling/task logs; `generate-topk_minmax.log.gz` records generation. `/tmp/rp-bench-r3/matched-report/report.md` is the supplied statistical summary. No sampled CPU profile or per-query critical-path trace is provided. Runtime diagnostics **are** recorded: GC count/ms and accumulated driver compiler-thread ms; they cannot establish a specific causal attribution on their own.
- Timings in the ledger are medians over 16 timed records. Ratios use the harness definition read from [summarize.py:230](/home/unik/Coding/rust/rp-work/bench/benchmarks/iceberg-runtime-pruning-detailed/summarize.py:230): median the two repetitions in each round, form baseline-duration/candidate-duration, geometrically average eight paired round ratios, and bootstrap the paired rounds 4,000 times with seed 1 for an exploratory 95% percentile interval. This was recomputed by independent text processing, without importing/executing the harness. Labels such as cand_on/base_on denote **speedup**, so a value above 1 means the candidate is faster. The estimate need not equal pooled baseline median / pooled candidate median; CSV includes both. Other ratio columns use the same estimator. Eight rounds and many queries do not give multiple-comparison-adjusted certainty.
- Order in all tables/sections is lowest paired cand_on/base_on speedup first. Metric vectors use **BO / BN / CO / CN** = baseline_off / baseline_on / candidate_off / candidate_on. Bytes and emitted rows sum native Iceberg scans, including dimensions. They are not final result rows or rows decoded. Per-scan CSV entries separate fact from dimensions. Runtime file counters count **file tasks/splits**, not unique files; `num_splits` counts planned tasks, not tasks that successfully read data. Baseline lacks the new file/RG/refresh counters: absent is `n/a`, never fabricated zero. No decoded-row or page-pruned counters are exposed in these result schemas; source supports page selection, but observed page counts are **unknown**. Dedicated dynamic_filter_join_rows_* counters cover the batch helper only; zero can mean reader filtering instead. Native time counters use nanoseconds of aggregate operator/task work, which can overlap; they are not query wall time. Spill metrics preserve zero versus absent.
- Code citations below reference the **current heads**, while measurements belong to the older candidate. `9cccd0676` is in the measured history and was reverted by `72981e4c3`; it affected materialized-shuffle eligibility, not broadcast reader paths. Despite it, the recorded shuffled-hash query evaluates 16M reducer rows in both on variants. Current Iceberg preserves floating binary/set row filters ([crates/iceberg/src/arrow/reader/pipeline.rs:964](/home/unik/Coding/rust/rp-work/iceberg-nanfix/crates/iceberg/src/arrow/reader/pipeline.rs:964)); this does not expand Comet's float runtime eligibility. Delete-load cancellation/retry fixes ([crates/iceberg/src/arrow/caching_delete_file_loader.rs:59](/home/unik/Coding/rust/rp-work/iceberg-nanfix/crates/iceberg/src/arrow/caching_delete_file_loader.rs:59)) affect abandoned loads/shared waiters; no artifact measures cancelled load work, so no gain is assigned to them.
- The prior suite report is not authoritative: its stated baseline/dependency SHAs disagree with the resolver above; unchanged plans/bytes do not prove zero CPU regressions; and claims that JIT or shared-runner jitter explain the entire difference exceed the available evidence. This report uses the actual records and labels mechanisms versus hypotheses explicitly.

## B. One-line RCA for every query

- **topk10__f_str — under-delivers**: 16M range-sorted rows; id is fixed-width lexical k + ten-digit raw id; selective sorted string key opportunity rejected by type gates; fact scan 573.50 MiB, 16,000,000 emitted rows, 0 file tasks / 0 RGs pruned.
- **both__f_long — expected-parity**: 16M range-sorted rows with long id; combined extrema, grouping, or ordinary aggregation has no eligible single-extremum bound; fact scan 25.57 MiB, 16,000,000 emitted rows, 0 file tasks / 0 RGs pruned.
- **topk10_two_keys__f_nan — expected-parity**: 16M range-sorted raw rows; double id has NaN every 97th row, NULL every 89th remaining row, -0.0 every 83rd remaining row; float/NaN keys fail runtime type eligibility; NaN matches spread across files make numeric-only pruning unsafe; fact scan 588.15 MiB, 16,000,000 emitted rows, 0 file tasks / 0 RGs pruned.
- **topk10_desc__f_str — under-delivers**: 16M range-sorted rows; id is fixed-width lexical k + ten-digit raw id; selective sorted string key opportunity rejected by type gates; fact scan 573.50 MiB, 16,000,000 emitted rows, 0 file tasks / 0 RGs pruned.
- **topk100_filtered__f_unsorted — under-delivers**: 16M integer rows, 16 randomly distributed files, randomized row order; file/group key bounds broadly overlap; value % 3 = 0 retains every generated row; Spark filter blocks direct-scan-only Top-K fusion; fact scan 720.42 MiB, 16,000,000 emitted rows, 0 file tasks / 0 RGs pruned.
- **topk10_by_id1__f_sorted — expected-parity**: 16M integer rows range-partitioned and sorted across 16 files; value = 3 * raw and k2 = raw % 1000; computed id + 1 is outside direct-column Top-K eligibility; fact scan 605.77 MiB, 16,000,000 emitted rows, 0 file tasks / 0 RGs pruned.
- **topk10_by_payload__f_unsorted — expected-parity**: 16M integer rows, 16 randomly distributed files, randomized row order; file/group key bounds broadly overlap; random hash payload has unsupported string bounds and little physical clustering; fact scan 659.77 MiB, 16,000,000 emitted rows, 0 file tasks / 0 RGs pruned.
- **topk100_filtered__f_sorted — under-delivers**: 16M integer rows range-partitioned and sorted across 16 files; value = 3 * raw and k2 = raw % 1000; value % 3 = 0 retains every generated row; Spark filter blocks direct-scan-only Top-K fusion; fact scan 621.66 MiB, 16,000,000 emitted rows, 0 file tasks / 0 RGs pruned.
- **both__f_nan — expected-parity**: 16M range-sorted raw rows; double id has NaN every 97th row, NULL every 89th remaining row, -0.0 every 83rd remaining row; float/NaN keys fail runtime type eligibility; NaN matches spread across files make numeric-only pruning unsafe; fact scan 33.23 MiB, 16,000,000 emitted rows, 0 file tasks / 0 RGs pruned.
- **both__f_overlap — expected-parity**: 16M integer rows, 16 overlapping files, each sorted internally across the full key range; combined extrema, grouping, or ordinary aggregation has no eligible single-extremum bound; fact scan 54.05 MiB, 16,000,000 emitted rows, 0 file tasks / 0 RGs pruned.
- **topk10_desc_two_keys__f_nan — expected-parity**: 16M range-sorted raw rows; double id has NaN every 97th row, NULL every 89th remaining row, -0.0 every 83rd remaining row; float/NaN keys fail runtime type eligibility; NaN matches spread across files make numeric-only pruning unsafe; fact scan 588.15 MiB, 16,000,000 emitted rows, 0 file tasks / 0 RGs pruned.
- **both__f_sorted — expected-parity**: 16M integer rows range-partitioned and sorted across 16 files; value = 3 * raw and k2 = raw % 1000; combined extrema, grouping, or ordinary aggregation has no eligible single-extremum bound; fact scan 50.85 MiB, 16,000,000 emitted rows, 0 file tasks / 0 RGs pruned.
- **max__f_unsorted__filtered — under-delivers**: 16M integer rows, 16 randomly distributed files, randomized row order; file/group key bounds broadly overlap; value % 3 = 0 retains every generated row; Spark filter blocks reader attachment (scalar spark_modulo is outside safety whitelist); fact scan 120.80 MiB, 16,000,000 emitted rows, 0 file tasks / 0 RGs pruned.

- **both__f_nulls — expected-parity**: 16M range-sorted rows; id NULL every tenth raw row, value = 3 * raw, secondary value tie-break for Top-K; combined extrema, grouping, or ordinary aggregation has no eligible single-extremum bound; fact scan 47.30 MiB, 16,000,000 emitted rows, 0 file tasks / 0 RGs pruned.
- **topk10_desc_nulls_first_two_keys__f_nulls — under-delivers**: 16M range-sorted rows; id NULL every tenth raw row, value = 3 * raw, secondary value tie-break for Top-K; NULLS FIRST with repeated null primary keys records attachment but zero reader predicate tasks; null-threshold/secondary-key translation is the likely gap; fact scan 602.18 MiB, 16,000,000 emitted rows, 0 file tasks / 0 RGs pruned; off/off captures local Top-K fusion benefit.
- **topk10_two_keys__f_nulls — under-delivers**: 16M range-sorted rows; id NULL every tenth raw row, value = 3 * raw, secondary value tie-break for Top-K; NULLS FIRST with repeated null primary keys records attachment but zero reader predicate tasks; null-threshold/secondary-key translation is the likely gap; fact scan 602.18 MiB, 16,000,000 emitted rows, 0 file tasks / 0 RGs pruned; off/off captures local Top-K fusion benefit.
- **topk100_by_k2id_two_keys__f_sorted — under-delivers**: 16M integer rows range-partitioned and sorted across 16 files; value = 3 * raw and k2 = raw % 1000; k2 repeats 0..999 in every file/group; first-key bound emits fewer rows but full bytes remain; no secondary-id bound pushed to the reader; fact scan 607.21 MiB, 889,903 emitted rows, 0 file tasks / 0 RGs pruned.

## C. Per-query deep dives

Each subsection is triggered by an interval reaching below parity, an on/off point estimate below parity, an under-delivery opportunity, a >10x estimate, or unequal coverage. An overlapping interval is explicitly distinguished from one entirely below 1.0. Proposals below are future work; none were implemented or run.

### topk10__f_str

**Classification/trigger:** under-delivers; 95% CI overlaps below 1.0 (exploratory); candidate on/off point estimate or pooled-median speedup below 1.0; under-delivery opportunity.

**Proven observations:** 16M range-sorted rows; id is fixed-width lexical k + ten-digit raw id. Captured SQL: `SELECT id, value, payload FROM bench.db.f_str ORDER BY id LIMIT 10`. Total medians BO/BN/CO/CN = 790.02 / 776.14 / 774.92 / 774.89 ms. Paired CN/BN = 0.994 [0.967, 1.022]; CN/CO = 0.989 [0.961, 1.019]; CO/BO = 1.024 [0.998, 1.054]. selective sorted string key opportunity rejected by type gates; fact scan 573.50 MiB, 16,000,000 emitted rows, 0 file tasks / 0 RGs pruned.

**Metrics:** fact bytes BO/BN/CO/CN = 601,353,562 / 601,353,562 / 601,353,562 / 601,353,562; fact emitted rows = 16,000,000 / 16,000,000 / 16,000,000 / 16,000,000. Reader refreshes = n/a / n/a / 0 / 0; decoder rebuilds = n/a / n/a / 0 / 0. Batch-helper eval ns = n/a / n/a / n/a / n/a; attached/skipped counters CN = n/a / n/a. Spill bytes = 0 / 0 / 0 / 0. Native Top-K attached/skipped CN = n/a / n/a.

**Equivalence/plan:** all 64 timed results and 128 warmups for this query are exact against Spark; oracle final rows=10, checksum `a879b8387cefd7072a73af895c510dbef1dc97e2b085d29ab146e71390920faf`. Captured scan metadata/projection/filter identities agree after generated IDs are normalized where coverage is equal. Normalized captured on-plans equal: True; operator count differences [baseline_on, candidate_on]: `{}`. No operator differences in the normalized captured tree; internal reader/runtime wrappers are not visible in this Spark tree. Plans: [baseline](/tmp/rp-bench-r3/matched-topk_minmax/plans/topk_minmax/baseline_on/6b545a7c68517e8891c240bded6a4d90c9e716ce02fb805d9fbbe1cfd28663a3.txt), [candidate](/tmp/rp-bench-r3/matched-topk_minmax/plans/topk_minmax/candidate_on/6b545a7c68517e8891c240bded6a4d90c9e716ce02fb805d9fbbe1cfd28663a3.txt).

**Hypothesis and current code path:** The current producer/field gates prevent any string/decimal domain reaching Iceberg; this is a capability gap rather than evidence of failed publication after attachment. [native/core/src/execution/operators/dynamic_filter/join.rs:343](/home/unik/Coding/rust/rp-work/comet/native/core/src/execution/operators/dynamic_filter/join.rs:343); [native/core/src/execution/operators/iceberg_scan.rs:160](/home/unik/Coding/rust/rp-work/comet/native/core/src/execution/operators/iceberg_scan.rs:160); [native/core/src/execution/operators/dynamic_filter/topk.rs:74](/home/unik/Coding/rust/rp-work/comet/native/core/src/execution/operators/dynamic_filter/topk.rs:74). Iceberg already hashes Utf8 IN/NOT IN at [crates/iceberg/src/arrow/reader/predicate_visitor.rs:242](/home/unik/Coding/rust/rp-work/iceberg-nanfix/crates/iceberg/src/arrow/reader/predicate_visitor.rs:242), but Comet runtime producers, field mapping and literal translation must all be extended together.

**Competing explanations/limits:** For equality-delete tables, hash NOT IN dominates the apparent win. DISTINCT/build conversion and Spark string aggregates also cost time. Wide-range string builds cannot skip whole files even after type support.

**Measured versus current heads:** 9cccd0676 targets shuffle-probe eligibility; it does not solve this current broadcast/Top-K/control mechanism. The later float guard and delete-load cancellation address other predicate/lifecycle cases and do not add this missing capability. These integer/string/decimal no-delete paths have no direct measured benefit from those fixes.

**Concrete proposal:** Add matching Utf8 runtime key/Datum support first, with conservative collation and bounds; separately add same-scale Decimal128 support with checked scale/precision conversion. Keep floats excluded. For string MIN/MAX, also address the existing Spark SortAggregate fallback and aggregate type gate; adding scan-field mapping alone will not create a native extrema producer.

**Risk and expected gain:** Medium/high correctness risk (collation, truncated bounds, decimal coercion, null semantics). Narrow sorted 16M cases have a conditional ceiling of roughly 0.6–1.2 seconds/query, by analogy with integer queries; scattered and 1M equality-delete cases have smaller/uncertain savings.

**Future validation (not executed):** target queries `topk10__f_str`, `join_inner__f_str__dim_128`, `join_inner__f_str__dim_10k`, `join_inner__f_dec__dim_128`, `join_inner__f_dec__dim_10k`, `topk10_desc__f_str`, `join_inner__f_eq_deletes_str__dim_128`, `topk10__f_eq_deletes_str`. Reuse identical hashed fixture files and the four-variant/eight-balanced-JVM-round protocol; require exact Spark results and matching intended plans, record bytes/row-group/row-filter work and planning/execution separately, and judge both paired intervals and absolute milliseconds. Add page/decoded-row and native eligibility/CPU diagnostics where the proposed mechanism depends on unrecorded work. For cancellation/cache changes, separately exercise early stream drop with concurrent shared-delete waiters; a completed-query timing comparison alone is insufficient.

**Hypothesis and current code path:** Unsupported floating runtime key gates deliberately leave these cases unfiltered; this is expected conservative behavior, not evidence of a current-head NaN correctness failure. [native/core/src/execution/operators/dynamic_filter/join.rs:343](/home/unik/Coding/rust/rp-work/comet/native/core/src/execution/operators/dynamic_filter/join.rs:343); [native/core/src/execution/operators/dynamic_filter/topk.rs:74](/home/unik/Coding/rust/rp-work/comet/native/core/src/execution/operators/dynamic_filter/topk.rs:74); current row-filter guard [crates/iceberg/src/arrow/reader/pipeline.rs:964](/home/unik/Coding/rust/rp-work/iceberg-nanfix/crates/iceberg/src/arrow/reader/pipeline.rs:964).

**Competing explanations/limits:** NaN matches appear throughout files; Spark sorts NaN above numeric values and uses SQL equivalence for signed zero/NaN. A numeric-only domain would be wrong. Small timing differences need repeated observations, not a profiling assertion.

**Measured versus current heads:** 9cccd0676 and its revert do not change this broadcast/Top-K float eligibility. The later floating row-filter guard prevents unsafe elision of floating binary/set filters; these queries use no supported floating runtime producer and recorded results already match Spark. This suite does not validate every guarded predicate shape. Delete-load cancellation has no delete files here.

**Concrete proposal:** Keep conservative float eligibility. If extending it later, carry explicit NaN/null/signed-zero semantics through producer, bounds and row kernels before cost tuning.

**Risk and expected gain:** Medium/high correctness risk (collation, truncated bounds, decimal coercion, null semantics). ### topk10_desc_nulls_first_two_keys__f_nulls

**Classification/trigger:** under-delivers; selective Top-K with an attached producer but no reader predicate tasks; pooled-median candidate on/off speedup below parity (paired interval overlaps parity).

**Proven observations:** `SELECT id, value, payload FROM bench.db.f_nulls ORDER BY id DESC NULLS FIRST, value LIMIT 10`. The fixture has 16M rows and NULL id every tenth raw row; ties sort by value. Total medians BO/BN/CO/CN = 769.14 / 759.81 / 703.23 / 703.58 ms. CN/BN = 1.080 [1.060, 1.099]; CN/CO = 1.005; CO/BO = 1.077. All variants read 631,432,245 native bytes and emit 16M scan rows. Candidate-on attaches 5 local Top-K filters, but reader predicate tasks, file/RG pruning, refreshes and rebuilds all remain zero; recorded spill bytes are zero.

**Equivalence/plan:** All 64 timed results and 128 warmups are exact against Spark; 10 final rows, checksum `0a030b848db09b3c559e4a43d18726721e19cb45bd6216a1eb758731b0390fc5`. Coverage is equal. Candidate adds intended CometLocalTopK below the same global CometTakeOrderedAndProject; no scan fallback. Plans: [baseline](/tmp/rp-bench-r3/matched-topk_minmax/plans/topk_minmax/baseline_on/b5bf48bfa57b3b9ad4eee458a071209b482303a5b1687946b7768c31f09f2350.txt), [candidate](/tmp/rp-bench-r3/matched-topk_minmax/plans/topk_minmax/candidate_on/b5bf48bfa57b3b9ad4eee458a071209b482303a5b1687946b7768c31f09f2350.txt). Off/off improvement therefore includes local fusion independently of runtime pruning.

**Hypothesis/current code:** The completed threshold is a null id with a secondary value; current conservative extraction cannot express its useful null/secondary-key bound. [predicate.rs:138](/home/unik/Coding/rust/rp-work/comet/native/core/src/execution/operators/dynamic_filter/iceberg_reader/predicate.rs:138) handles narrowly recognized first-key disjunctions, and [predicate.rs:220](/home/unik/Coding/rust/rp-work/comet/native/core/src/execution/operators/dynamic_filter/iceberg_reader/predicate.rs:220) admits only non-null literals. The exact published native expression was not logged, so this mechanism remains a code-supported hypothesis.

**Competing explanations:** A declined or unavailable publication could also produce zero reader tasks. Nulls exist in every file, so a null-only bound would not skip whole files; secondary value pruning or late materialization is needed. The ~1.08x headline is already explained in part by off/off fusion, not observed reader pruning.

**Later fixes:** 9cccd0676/revert affect shuffled joins, and neither the floating row-filter guard nor delete-load cancellation adds lexicographic/null Top-K translation here. No delete files are involved.

**Concrete proposal, risk, gain:** Publish a conservative `id IS NULL AND value <= threshold` bound once the local heap is filled with null winners, retaining all ties needed for exact ordering. Prove NULLS FIRST, ascending/descending secondary directions, incomplete heap and equal-key behavior. Medium/high correctness risk; additional gain is unquantified, and a null-only bound offers little file pruning. The ~0.7-second full scan is opportunity, not a forecast.

**Future validation (not executed):** `topk10_desc_nulls_first_two_keys__f_nulls`, `topk10_two_keys__f_nulls`, `topk10_nulls_last_two_keys__f_nulls`, `topk10_desc_two_keys__f_nulls`, `topk100_by_k2id_two_keys__f_sorted`. Retain identical fixture files, exact Spark ordered results and the eight-balanced-round four-variant protocol. Capture the published expression/generation, reader predicate tasks, selected rows/pages, bytes and decoder work before claiming improvement.

### topk10_desc__f_eq_deletes

**Classification/trigger:** pruning-win; above 10x paired or pooled-median speedup.

**Proven observations:** 1M sampled rows (stride 16 across the 16M key range), 16 data files, one 4,096-key equality-delete file; 995,904 live rows; integer keys. Captured SQL: `SELECT id, value, payload FROM bench.db.f_eq_deletes ORDER BY id DESC LIMIT 10`. Total medians BO/BN/CO/CN = 1,003.44 / 1,001.99 / 127.87 / 96.03 ms. Paired CN/BN = 10.136 [9.655, 10.633]; CN/CO = 1.327 [1.254, 1.400]; CO/BO = 7.673 [7.516, 7.811]. supported bound reaches the reader and rejects file/group or row work; fact scan 22.75 MiB, 499,231 emitted rows, 8 file tasks / 0 RGs pruned; large cross-engine gain includes faster equality deletes.

**Metrics:** fact bytes BO/BN/CO/CN = 47,477,184 / 47,477,184 / 47,477,184 / 23,852,487; fact emitted rows = 995,904 / 995,904 / 995,904 / 499,231. Reader refreshes = n/a / n/a / 0 / 0; decoder rebuilds = n/a / n/a / 0 / 0. Batch-helper eval ns = n/a / n/a / n/a / n/a; attached/skipped counters CN = n/a / n/a. Spill bytes = 0 / 0 / 0 / 0. Native Top-K attached/skipped CN = 8 / 0.

**Equivalence/plan:** all 64 timed results and 128 warmups for this query are exact against Spark; oracle final rows=10, checksum `41b89810161945267371f0e1b2b877016bc042e7c4d7b0025f2f2c4076173b45`. Captured scan metadata/projection/filter identities agree after generated IDs are normalized where coverage is equal. Normalized captured on-plans equal: False; operator count differences [baseline_on, candidate_on]: `{"CometLocalTopKExec": [0, 1]}`. The extra CometLocalTopK is the intended local fusion enabling reader feedback; the final global Top-K remains. Off/off captures this structural difference as well as other engine changes. Plans: [baseline](/tmp/rp-bench-r3/matched-topk_minmax/plans/topk_minmax/baseline_on/906bc74829d7127b4f020648b448c2e59675b9ce036f9ebff5226410fa562f54.txt), [candidate](/tmp/rp-bench-r3/matched-topk_minmax/plans/topk_minmax/candidate_on/906bc74829d7127b4f020648b448c2e59675b9ce036f9ebff5226410fa562f54.txt).

**Hypothesis and current code path:** Supported bounds remove substantial file/group/row work; any >10x headline must also account for local Top-K fusion and equality-delete acceleration when present. Join connection [native/core/src/execution/operators/dynamic_filter/join.rs:88](/home/unik/Coding/rust/rp-work/comet/native/core/src/execution/operators/dynamic_filter/join.rs:88); Top-K fusion [spark/src/main/scala/org/apache/spark/sql/comet/CometLocalTopKExec.scala:62](/home/unik/Coding/rust/rp-work/comet/spark/src/main/scala/org/apache/spark/sql/comet/CometLocalTopKExec.scala:62); ordering [native/core/src/execution/operators/iceberg_scan.rs:731](/home/unik/Coding/rust/rp-work/comet/native/core/src/execution/operators/iceberg_scan.rs:731); whole-file pruning [crates/iceberg/src/arrow/reader/pipeline.rs:181](/home/unik/Coding/rust/rp-work/iceberg-nanfix/crates/iceberg/src/arrow/reader/pipeline.rs:181); live refresh [crates/iceberg/src/arrow/reader/runtime_stream.rs:125](/home/unik/Coding/rust/rp-work/iceberg-nanfix/crates/iceberg/src/arrow/reader/runtime_stream.rs:125); equality-delete membership [crates/iceberg/src/arrow/reader/predicate_visitor.rs:242](/home/unik/Coding/rust/rp-work/iceberg-nanfix/crates/iceberg/src/arrow/reader/predicate_visitor.rs:242).

**Measured versus current heads:** 9cccd0676 targets shuffle probes and cannot explain this broadcast/scan/Top-K path. The later float guard is not the key type here. Later delete-load cancellation/retry can improve abandoned-task resource cleanup and shared-waiter correctness, but these completed-query records contain no cancellation counter or leaked-work measurement; no timing change is asserted.

**Risk and expected gain:** Medium concurrency/lifecycle risk. Additional gain is limited by residual scan/task work and is unquantified; headline >10x is already measured and must not be reused as a forecast.

**Measured versus current heads:** 9cccd0676 targets shuffle probes and cannot explain this broadcast/scan/Top-K path. The later float guard is not the key type here. Later delete-load cancellation/retry can improve abandoned-task resource cleanup and shared-waiter correctness, but these completed-query records contain no cancellation counter or leaked-work measurement; no timing change is asserted.

**Concrete proposal:** Add matching Utf8 runtime key/Datum support first, with conservative collation and bounds; separately add same-scale Decimal128 support with checked scale/precision conversion. Keep floats excluded. For string MIN/MAX, also address the existing Spark SortAggregate fallback and aggregate type gate; adding scan-field mapping alone will not create a native extrema producer.

**Risk and expected gain:** Medium/high correctness risk (collation, truncated bounds, decimal coercion, null semantics). Narrow sorted 16M cases have a conditional ceiling of roughly 0.6–1.2 seconds/query, by analogy with integer queries; scattered and 1M equality-delete cases have smaller/uncertain savings.
